Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   No approximations are allowed for these opcodes.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel. The body lives in the ProgramSlot subprogram table,\n   244\t    // NOT inline after this opcode. block_id + allocator config travel in the\n   245\t    // matching StateInitData (ForeachAlloc), keyed by state_id. Generalizes\n   246\t    // POLY_BEGIN/POLY_END; see cedar/opcodes/foreach_event.hpp.\n   247\t    FOREACH_EVENT = 215,\n   248\t\n   249\t    // BLOCK_BIND (216) \u2014 PRD prd-runtime-functions-control-flow \u00a74.2.\n   250\t    // Extra-argument carrier for a BLOCK_CALL whose target shared `fn` has more\n   251\t    // than 5 parameters (BLOCK_CALL's inputs[0..4] cover only the first 5). One\n   252\t    // BLOCK_BIND binds one logical parameter slot >= 5: `rate` = slot index\n   253\t    // (5..MAX_SHARED_FN_PARAMS-1), `inputs[0]` = source buffer. A contiguous run\n   254\t    // of BLOCK_BINDs precedes its BLOCK_CALL; the VM dispatch loop scans the run\n   255\t    // and hands it to execute_block_call. Dispatch-loop opcode \u2014 never reaches\n   256\t    // execute().\n   257\t    BLOCK_BIND = 216,        // dispatch: bind logical param slot >=5 for BLOCK_CALL\n   258\t\n   259\t    // Pattern Event Arrays (217) \u2014 PRD prd-pattern-event-arrays.\n   260\t    // Packs the active sequence event's chord notes (evt.values[]) into a\n   261\t    // single buffer for the DynArray data path: samples 0..num_values-1 hold\n   262\t    // the notes, the rest are 0. rate: 0 = raw Hz (freqs(e)), 1 = MIDI via\n   263\t    // freq\u2192note conversion (notes(e)). inputs[1] = external clock (0xFFFF =\n   264\t    // internal; matches SEQPAT_FIELD's clock slot). state_id must match the\n   265\t    // SEQPAT_QUERY that filled the state.\n   266\t    SEQPAT_VALUES = 217,\n   267\t\n   268\t    // Runtime Event-Stream Transforms (218-219) \u2014 PRD prd-runtime-event-\n   269\t    // transforms. Each reads an upstream event source's\n   271\t    // OutputEvents (SequenceState or MidiQueueState, via resolve_output_events)\n   272\t    // and publishes its own transformed OutputEvents into a transform-owned\n   273\t    // SequenceState. This is the A/B oracle for the equality gate \u2014 the stimuli are the\n    18\t// same ones the timing path uses (one source of truth).\n    19\t\n    20\t#include \"cedar/vm/vm.hpp\"\n    21\t#include \"cedar/vm/instruction.hpp\"\n    22\t#include \"cedar/dsp/simd.hpp\"\n    23\t\n    24\t#include <algorithm>\n    25\t#include <array>\n    26\t#include <chrono>\n    27\t#include <cmath>\n    28\t#include <cstdint>\n    29\t#include <cstdio>\n    30\t#include <cstring>\n    31\t#include <fstream>\n    32\t#include <functional>\n    33\t#include <memory>\n    34\t#include <random>\n    35\t#include <string>\n    36\t#include <string_view>\n    37\t#include <vector>\n    38\t\n    39\tusing namespace cedar;\n    40\t\n    41\tnamespace {\n    42\t\n    43\tconstexpr std::size_t INSTANCES = 64;\n    44\tconstexpr std::size_t STIM_BLOCKS = 256;\n    45\tconstexpr std::uint16_t IN_BASE = 0;    // stimulus buffers 0..4\n    46\tconstexpr std::uint16_t OUT_BASE = 8;   // outputs at 8 + 2k (stereo pairs)\n    47\tconstexpr float SR = 48000.0f;\n    48\tconstexpr std::uint16_t NONE = 0xFFFF;\n    49\t\n    50\t// Stimulus for one input slot at absolute sample index n (0..STIM_BLOCKS*BLOCK_SIZE).\n    51\tusing Stim = std::function<float(std::size_t n)>;\n    52\t\n    53\t// --seed N (N != 0) switches every stimulus to a randomised \"hidden\" variant\n    54\t// (PRD \u00a76 / research P5): the full verify picks a fresh seed per call and\n    55\t// dumps baseline and candidate with it, so a kernel cannot be tuned to the\n    56\t// fixed stimuli the quick check and the timing path use.\n    57\tstd::uint32_t g_seed = 0;\n    58\tstd::mt19937 g_hidden_rng;  // draws per-stimulus variations in hidden mode\n    59\t\n    60\tfloat frac(std::size_t n, std::size_t period) {\n    61\t    return static_cast<float>(n % period) / static_cast<float>(period);\n    62\t}\n    63\t\n    64\t// Audio-rate signal input: seeded noise \u2192 log sine sweep \u2192 impulse train \u2192\n    65\t// ramp, a quarter of the stimulus window each (PRD \u00a74.3 fixed stimuli).\n    66\tstruct AudioStim {\n    67\t    float gain;\n    68\t    std::vector<float> noise;\n    69\t    explicit AudioStim(float g) : gain(g), noise(STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE) {\n    70\t        std::mt19937 rng(g_seed ? g_seed : 0xCEDA5u);\n    71\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> d(-1.0f, 1.0f);\n    72\t        for (auto& x : noise) x = d(rng);\n    73\t        if (g_seed) make_hidden(rng);\n    74\t    }\n    75\t    // Hidden mode: 8 segments, each a random choice of noise at a random\n    76\t    // gain (from near-denormal to 4x the nominal level), sparse random\n    77\t    // impulses, DC steps, or silence. Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t    float operator()(std::size_t n) const {\n   102\t        if (g_seed) return noise[n];\n   103\t        constexpr std::size_t total = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE;\n   104\t        constexpr std::size_t quarter = total / 4;\n   105\t        std::size_t seg = n / quarter, k = n % quarter;\n   106\t        float t = static_cast<float>(k) / static_cast<float>(quarter);\n   107\t        switch (seg) {\n   108\t            case 0: return gain * noise[n];\n   109\t            case 1: {\n   110\t                // 20 Hz \u2192 20 kHz exponential sweep, phase-integrated\n   111\t                double f0 = 20.0, f1 = 20000.0, T = double(quarter) / double(SR);\n   112\t                double tt = double(k) / double(SR);\n   113\t                double kk = std::log(f1 / f0) / T;\n   114\t                double ph = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * f0 * (std::exp(kk * tt) - 1.0) / kk;\n   115\t                return gain * static_cast<float>(std::sin(ph));\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            case 2: return (k % 997 == 0) ? gain : 0.0f;\n   118\t            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n   119\t        }\n   120\t    }\n   121\t};\n   122\t\n   123\tStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n   124\t    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n   125\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n   126\t        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n   127\t        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n   128\t        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n   129\t    }\n   130\t    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n   131\t}\n   132\tStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n   133\t\n   134\tstruct Case {\n   135\t    const char* name;\n   136\t    Opcode op;\n   137\t    std::vector<Stim> inputs;      // wired to inputs[0..]; remaining slots NONE\n   138\t    std::uint16_t first_unused = 0; // inputs >= this index are NONE\n   139\t};\n   140\t\n   141\tstd::vector<Case> make_cases() {\n   142\t    AudioStim audio(1.0f), hot(3.0f);\n   143\t    constexpr std::size_t P = 48000; }\n   207\t    void block(std::size_t b) { load(b); run(); }\n   208\t};\n   209\t\n   210\tstd::string read_first_line(const char* path, std::string_view key = {}) {\n   211\t    std::ifstream f(path);\n   212\t    std::string line;\n   213\t    while (std::getline(f, line)) {\n   214\t        if (key.empty()) return line;\n   215\t        if (line.rfind(key, 0) == 0) {\n   216\t            auto p = line.find(':');\n   217\t            return p == std::string::npos ? line : line.substr(p + 2);\n   218\t        }\n   219\t    }\n   220\t    return \"unknown\";\n   221\t}\n   222\t\n   223\tstd::string json_escape(std::string s) {\n   224\t    std::string o;\n   225\t    for (char ch : s) {\n   226\t        if (ch == '\"' || ch == '\\\\') o += '\\\\';\n   227\t        o += ch;\n   228\t    }\n   229\t    return o;\n   230\t}\n   231\t\n   232\tint usage() {\n   233\t    std::fprintf(stderr,\n   234\t        \"usage: cedar_bench --list\\n\"\n   235\t        \"       cedar_bench --opcode NAME [--reps N] [--warmup N] [--json]\\n\"\n   236\t        \"       cedar_bench --opcode NAME --dump FILE [--blocks N]\\n\"\n   237\t        \"       --scalar forces the scalar fallback path (A/B the dispatch)\\n\"\n   238\t        \"       --seed N (N != 0) uses randomised hidden stimuli\\n\");\n   239\t    return 2;\n   240\t}\n   241\t\n   242\t}  // namespace\n   243\t\n   244\tint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n   245\t    std::string opcode, dump;\n   246\t    std::size_t reps = 500, warmup = 200, blocks = 1024;\n   247\t    bool json = false, list = false;\n   248\t    for (int i = 1; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\\n        out[i] = a[i] * b[i];\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// DIV: out = in0 / in1 (safe division, returns 0 for 0/0)\\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\\ninline void op_div(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\\n    float* out = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\\n    const float* a = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\\n    const float* b = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\\n\\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\\n        out[i] = (b[i] != 0.0f) ?   "content": "   180\t\n   181\tRemote and WASM legs run **after** acceptance, on the winners, not inside the\n   182\tper-iteration loop.\n   183\t\n   184\t---\n   185\t\n   186\t## 5. Architecture\n   187\t\n   188\t### 5.1 Runtime dispatch\n   189\t\n   190\t```cpp\n   191\t// cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd.hpp  (new)\n   192\tnamespace cedar::simd {\n   193\tenum class Isa { Scalar, Sse2, Avx2, Neon, Wasm128 };\n   194\tIsa active();              // resolved once at static-init, never in the audio path\n   195\tbool has(Isa) noexcept;\n   196\t}\n   197\t```\n   198\t\n   199\tEach optimised opcode keeps its scalar body and adds one or more kernels:\n   200\t\n   201\t```cpp\n   202\t// cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/arithmetic.hpp\n   203\t[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   204\t    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n   205\t#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n   206\t    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n   207\t#endif\n   208\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];   // fallback stays\n   209\t}\n   210\t```\n   211\t\n   212\tConstraints the loop must respect:\n   213\t\n   214\t- The scalar body is **never deleted** \u2014 it is the oracle and the fallback.\n   215\t- No allocation, no syscalls, no locks in a kernel (the zero-alloc trap enforces).\n   216\t- CPU feature detection runs once at static-init; never per block, never per sample.\n   217\t- Buffers are already 32-byte aligned; kernels may assume it and must assert it\n   218\t  in debug builds.\n   219\t\n   220\t### 5.2 Repository layout (new)\n   221\t\n   222\t```\n   223\tscripts/autoresearch/\n   224\t  run.py              # driver: (model, opcode) \u2192 N iterations \u2192 JSONL\n   225\t  backends/\n   226\t    claude_code.py    # claude -p, parses usage JSON\n   227\t    openrouter.py     # GLM / DeepSeek, reads usage field\n   228\t    local_openai.py   # LocalAI / Ollama, OpenAI-compatible\n   229\t  verify.sh           # build + all gates \u2192 verdict JSON on stdout\n   230\t  bench.sh            # wraps cedar_bench, medians, noise band\n   231\t  report.py           # JSONL \u2192 markdown table + chart PNG\n   232\t  prompts/\n   233\t    optimize_opcode.md\n   234\t  runs/               # gitignored; one dir per run\n   235\tcedar/benchmarks/\n   236\t  bench_opcodes.cpp   # cedar_bench target\n   237\t```\n   238\t\n   239\t### 5.3 Benchmark harness\n   240\t\n   241\t`cedar_bench` is a plain C++ target \u2014 no new dependency, per the repo's\n   242\tzero-dependency habit:\n   243\t\n   244\t```\n   245\t./build/release/bin/cedar_bench --opcode op_mul --reps 2000 --warmup 200 --json\n   246\t{\"opcode\":\"op_mul\",\"ns_per_block_median\":41.2,\"ns_p10\":40.7,\"ns_p90\":42.9,\"reps\":2000,\n   247\t \"isa\":\"avx2\",\"cpu\":\"...\",\"governor\":\"performance\"}\n   248\t```\n   249\t\n   250\t- Drives one opcode over a fixed program through the VM, steady state.\n   251\t- Reports median and p10/p90; the **noise band** is `(p90 \u2212 p10) / median` from a\n   252\t  baseline-vs-baseline run. A speedup inside that band is not a speedup.\n   253\t- Records CPU model and scaling governor; refuses to gate (warns and marks the\n   254\t  run `unstable`) if the governor is not `performance`.\n   255\t\n   256\t### 5.4 Log schema\n   257\t\n   258\tPer iteration, appended to `runs/<run-id>/iterations.jsonl`:\n   259\t\n   260\t```json\n   261\t{\"run_id\":\"2026-09-28T14:03Z_opus5_op_distort_tanh\",\"iteration\":3,\n   262\t \"model\":\"claude-opus-5\",\"provider\":\"claude-code\",\n   263\t \"opcode\":\"op_distort_tanh\",\"isa_target\":\"avx2\",\n   264\t \"tokens\":{\"input\":18422,\"output\":2811,\"cached\":12000},\n   265\t \"cost_usd\":0.41,\"wall_clock_s\":96.3,\"gpu_s\":null,\n   266\t \"verdict\":\"accept\",\"reject_reason\":null,\n   267\t \"gates\":{\"build\":\"pass\",\"equality\":\"approx\",\"null_test_dbfs\":-112.4,\n   268\t          \"unit\":\"pass\",\"zero_alloc\":\"pass\",\"experiment\":\"pass\"},\n   269\t \"bench\":{\"baseline_ns\":118.7,\"candidate_ns\":41.9,\"speedup\":2.83,\"noise_band\":0.031},\n   270\t \"patch_sha\":\"...\",\"branch\":\"autoresearch/opus5-distort-tanh\"}\n   271\t```\n   272\t\n   273\tPer run, `runs/<run-id>/summary.json`: totals (tokens, USD, wall clock, GPU\n   274\tseconds), best speedup, iteration index of first accept, accept/reject counts.\n   275\t\n   276\t### 5.5 Anti-cheat\n   277\t\n   278\tThe loop measures the thing it optimises, so the model must not be able to touch\n   279\tthe measurement. `verify.sh` enforces a **write allowlist**:\n   280\t\n   281\t- Writable: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`,\n   282\t  `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`.\n   283\t- Everything else \u2014 benchmarks, tests, experiments, budgets, CMake \u2014 is restored\n   284\t  from a pristine checkout before every verify. A patch touching them is an\n   285\t  automatic reject, logged as `reject_reason: \"out-of-allowlist edit\"`.\n   286\t\n   287\tThis is also a talk beat: the first thing you defend against is the optimiser\n   288\toptimising the scoreboard.\n   289\t\n   290\t---\n   291\t\n   292\t## 6. Edge Cases\n   293\t\n   294\t1. **Benchmark noise / thermal drift.** Long runs heat the machine and slow late\n   295\t   iterations. Mitigation: interleave baseline re-measurement every iteration\n   296\t   (speedup is always ratio-to-a-fresh-baseline, never to a number from an hour\n   297\t   ago); mark runs `unstable` when the fresh baseline drifts > 10 % from the\n   298\t   pinned one.\n   299\t2. **Model edits the benchmark or the tests.** Rejected by the allowlist (\u00a75.5)."
